#### 7.4.2 同步信号（Synchronization signals）

##### 7.4.2.1 物理层小区标识（Physical-layer cell identities）

**原文**

There are 1008 unique physical-layer cell identities given by

**译文**

共有 1008 个不同的物理层小区标识，由下式给出：

$$
N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{\mathrm{cell}}=3N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(1)}+N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(2)}.
$$

**原文**

where $N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(1)}\in\{0,1,\ldots,335\}$ and $N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(2)}\in\{0,1,2\}$.

**译文**

其中，$N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(1)}\in\{0,1,\ldots,335\}$，$N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(2)}\in\{0,1,2\}$。

##### 7.4.2.2 主同步信号（Primary synchronization signal）

**7.4.2.2.1 序列生成（Sequence generation）**

**原文**

The sequence $d_{\mathrm{PSS}}(n)$ for the primary synchronization signal is defined by

**译文**

主同步信号的序列 $d_{\mathrm{PSS}}(n)$ 定义为：

$$
\begin{aligned}d_{\mathrm{PSS}}(n)&=1-2x(m),\\m&=(n+43N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(2)})\bmod127,\\0&\leq n<127.\end{aligned}
$$

**原文**

where

**译文**

其中：

$$
x(i+7)=(x(i+4)+x(i))\bmod2.
$$

**原文**

and

**译文**

并且：

$$
[x(6)\ x(5)\ x(4)\ x(3)\ x(2)\ x(1)\ x(0)]=[1\ 1\ 1\ 0\ 1\ 1\ 0].
$$

**7.4.2.2.2 物理资源映射（Mapping to physical resources）**

**原文**

Mapping to physical resources is described in clause 7.4.3.

**译文**

物理资源映射在第 7.4.3 节中描述。

##### 7.4.2.3 辅同步信号（Secondary synchronization signal）

**7.4.2.3.1 序列生成（Sequence generation）**

**原文**

The sequence $d_{\mathrm{SSS}}(n)$ for the secondary synchronization signal is defined by

**译文**

辅同步信号的序列 $d_{\mathrm{SSS}}(n)$ 定义为：

$$
\begin{aligned}
d_{\mathrm{SSS}}(n)&=[1-2x_0((n+m_0)\bmod127)][1-2x_1((n+m_1)\bmod127)],\\
m_0&=15\left\lfloor\frac{N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(1)}}{112}\right\rfloor+5N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(2)},\\
m_1&=N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{(1)}\bmod112,\\0&\leq n<127.
\end{aligned}
$$

**原文**

where

**译文**

其中：

$$
\begin{aligned}x_0(i+7)&=(x_0(i+4)+x_0(i))\bmod2,\\x_1(i+7)&=(x_1(i+1)+x_1(i))\bmod2.\end{aligned}
$$

**原文**

and

**译文**

并且：

$$
\begin{aligned}
[x_0(6)\ x_0(5)\ x_0(4)\ x_0(3)\ x_0(2)\ x_0(1)\ x_0(0)]&=[0\ 0\ 0\ 0\ 0\ 0\ 1],\\
[x_1(6)\ x_1(5)\ x_1(4)\ x_1(3)\ x_1(2)\ x_1(1)\ x_1(0)]&=[0\ 0\ 0\ 0\ 0\ 0\ 1].
\end{aligned}
$$

**7.4.2.3.2 物理资源映射（Mapping to physical resources）**

**原文**

Mapping to physical resources is described in clause 7.4.3.

**译文**

物理资源映射在第 7.4.3 节中描述。

#### 7.4.3 SS/PBCH 块（SS/PBCH block）

##### 7.4.3.1 SS/PBCH 块的时频结构（Time-frequency structure of an SS/PBCH block）

**原文**

In the time domain, an SS/PBCH block consists of 4 OFDM symbols, numbered in increasing order from 0 to 3 within the SS/PBCH block, where PSS, SSS, and PBCH with associated DM-RS are mapped to symbols as given by Table 7.4.3.1-1.

**译文**

在时域，一个 SS/PBCH 块由 4 个 OFDM 符号组成，在 SS/PBCH 块内按递增顺序编号为 0 至 3。其中，PSS、SSS、PBCH 及其关联 DM-RS 按表 7.4.3.1-1 映射到相应符号。

**原文**

In the frequency domain, an SS/PBCH block consists of 240 contiguous subcarriers with the subcarriers numbered in increasing order from 0 to 239 within the SS/PBCH block. The quantities $k$ and $l$ represent the frequency and time indices, respectively, within one SS/PBCH block. The UE may assume that the complex-valued symbols corresponding to resource elements denoted as 'Set to 0' in Table 7.4.3.1-1 are set to zero. The quantity $v$ in Table 7.4.3.1-1 is given by $v=N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{\mathrm{cell}}\bmod4$. The quantity $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ is the subcarrier offset from subcarrier 0 in common resource block $N_{\mathrm{CRB}}^{\mathrm{SSB}}$ to subcarrier 0 of the SS/PBCH block, where the 4 least significant bits of $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ are given by the higher-layer parameter ssb-SubcarrierOffset and for SS/PBCH block type A the most significant bit of $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ is given by $a_{\bar{A}+5}$ in the PBCH payload as defined in subclause 7.1.1 of [4, TS 38.212]. If ssb-SubcarrierOffset is not provided, $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ is derived from the frequency difference between the SS/PBCH block and Point A.

**译文**

在频域，一个 SS/PBCH 块由 240 个连续子载波组成，在块内按递增顺序编号为 0 至 239。$k$ 和 $l$ 分别表示一个 SS/PBCH 块内的频率索引和时间索引。UE 可以假定表 7.4.3.1-1 中标记为 'Set to 0'（置为 0）的资源元素所对应的复值符号被置为零。表中的 $v$ 由 $v=N_{\mathrm{ID}}^{\mathrm{cell}}\bmod4$ 给出。$k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ 是从公共资源块 $N_{\mathrm{CRB}}^{\mathrm{SSB}}$ 的子载波 0 到 SS/PBCH 块的子载波 0 的子载波偏移，其中 $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ 的最低 4 位由高层参数 ssb-SubcarrierOffset 给出；对于 A 类型 SS/PBCH 块，其最高位由 [4, TS 38.212] 第 7.1.1 节定义的 PBCH 载荷中的 $a_{\bar{A}+5}$ 给出。如果未提供 ssb-SubcarrierOffset，则根据 SS/PBCH 块与 Point A 之间的频率差推导 $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$。

**原文**

The UE may assume that the complex-valued symbols corresponding to resource elements that are part of a common resource block partially or fully overlapping with an SS/PBCH block and not used for SS/PBCH transmission are set set to zero in the OFDM symbols where SS/PBCH block is transmitted.

**译文**

对于属于与 SS/PBCH 块部分或完全重叠的公共资源块、且未用于 SS/PBCH 传输的资源元素，UE 可以假定其对应的复值符号在发送 SS/PBCH 块的 OFDM 符号内被置为零。

**原文**

For an SS/PBCH block, the UE shall assume

- antenna port $p=4000$ is used for transmission of PSS, SSS and PBCH,
- the same cyclic prefix length and subcarrier spacing for the PSS, SSS, and PBCH,
- for SS/PBCH block type A, $\mu\in\{0,1\}$ and $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}\in\{0,1,2,\ldots,23\}$ with the quantities $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$, and $N_{\mathrm{CRB}}^{\mathrm{SSB}}$ expressed in terms of 15 kHz subcarrier spacing, and
- for SS/PBCH block type B, $\mu\in\{3,4\}$ and $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}\in\{0,1,2,\ldots,11\}$ with the quantity $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ expressed in terms of the subcarrier spacing provided by the higher-layer parameter subCarrierSpacingCommon and $N_{\mathrm{CRB}}^{\mathrm{SSB}}$ is expressed in terms of 60 kHz subcarrier spacing.

**译文**

对于一个 SS/PBCH 块，UE 应假定：

- 使用天线端口 $p=4000$ 传输 PSS、SSS 和 PBCH。
- PSS、SSS 和 PBCH 使用相同的循环前缀长度和子载波间隔。
- 对于 A 类型 SS/PBCH 块，$\mu\in\{0,1\}$，$k_{\mathrm{SSB}}\in\{0,1,2,\ldots,23\}$，其中 $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ 和 $N_{\mathrm{CRB}}^{\mathrm{SSB}}$ 按 15 kHz 子载波间隔表示。
- 对于 B 类型 SS/PBCH 块，$\mu\in\{3,4\}$，$k_{\mathrm{SSB}}\in\{0,1,2,\ldots,11\}$，其中 $k_{\mathrm{SSB}}$ 按高层参数 subCarrierSpacingCommon 提供的子载波间隔表示，$N_{\mathrm{CRB}}^{\mathrm{SSB}}$ 按 60 kHz 子载波间隔表示。

**原文**

The UE may assume that SS/PBCH blocks transmitted with the same block index on the same center frequency location are quasi co-located with respect to Doppler spread, Doppler shift, average gain, average delay, delay spread, and, when applicable, spatial Rx parameters. The UE shall not assume quasi co-location for any other SS/PBCH block transmissions.

**译文**

UE 可以假定在相同中心频率位置、以相同块索引传输的 SS/PBCH 块，在多普勒扩展、多普勒频移、平均增益、平均时延、时延扩展以及适用时的空间接收参数方面具有准共址关系。对于其他任何 SS/PBCH 块传输，UE 均不应假定具有准共址关系。

**原文**

Table 7.4.3.1-1: Resources within an SS/PBCH block for PSS, SSS, PBCH, and DM-RS for PBCH.

**译文**

表 7.4.3.1-1：SS/PBCH 块内用于 PSS、SSS、PBCH 及 PBCH DM-RS 的资源。

| Channel or signal / 信道或信号 | OFDM symbol number $l$ relative to the start of an SS/PBCH block / 相对于 SS/PBCH 块起点的 OFDM 符号编号 | Subcarrier number $k$ relative to the start of an SS/PBCH block / 相对于 SS/PBCH 块起点的子载波编号 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| PSS | 0 | 56, 57, …, 182 |
| SSS | 2 | 56, 57, …, 182 |
| Set to 0 / 置为 0 | 0 | 0, 1, …, 55, 183, 184, …, 239 |
| Set to 0 / 置为 0 | 2 | 48, 49, …, 55, 183, 184, …, 191 |
| PBCH | 1, 3 | 0, 1, …, 239 |
| PBCH | 2 | 0, 1, …, 47, 192, 193, …, 239 |
| DM-RS for PBCH / PBCH 的 DM-RS | 1, 3 | $0+v,4+v,8+v,\ldots,236+v$ |
| DM-RS for PBCH / PBCH 的 DM-RS | 2 | $0+v,4+v,8+v,\ldots,44+v$；$192+v,196+v,\ldots,236+v$ |

**7.4.3.1.1 SS/PBCH 块内的 PSS 映射（Mapping of PSS within an SS/PBCH block）**

**原文**

The UE shall assume the sequence of symbols $d_{\mathrm{PSS}}(0),\ldots,d_{\mathrm{PSS}}(126)$ constituting the primary synchronization signal to be scaled by a factor $\beta_{\mathrm{PSS}}$ to conform to the PSS power allocation specified in [5, TS 38.213] and mapped to resource elements $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$ in increasing order of $k$ where $k$ and $l$ are given by Table 7.4.3.1-1 and represent the frequency and time indices, respectively, within one SS/PBCH block.

**译文**

UE 应假定构成主同步信号的符号序列 $d_{\mathrm{PSS}}(0),\ldots,d_{\mathrm{PSS}}(126)$ 乘以缩放因子 $\beta_{\mathrm{PSS}}$，以满足 [5, TS 38.213] 规定的 PSS 功率分配，并按 $k$ 递增的顺序映射到资源元素 $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$。其中，$k$ 和 $l$ 由表 7.4.3.1-1 给出，分别表示一个 SS/PBCH 块内的频率索引和时间索引。

**7.4.3.1.2 SS/PBCH 块内的 SSS 映射（Mapping of SSS within an SS/PBCH block）**

**原文**

The UE shall assume the sequence of symbols $d_{\mathrm{SSS}}(0),\ldots,d_{\mathrm{SSS}}(126)$ constituting the secondary synchronization signal to be scaled by a factor $\beta_{\mathrm{SSS}}$ and mapped to resource elements $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$ in increasing order of $k$ where $k$ and $l$ are given by Table 7.4.3.1-1 and represent the frequency and time indices, respectively, within one SS/PBCH block.

**译文**

UE 应假定构成辅同步信号的符号序列 $d_{\mathrm{SSS}}(0),\ldots,d_{\mathrm{SSS}}(126)$ 乘以缩放因子 $\beta_{\mathrm{SSS}}$，并按 $k$ 递增的顺序映射到资源元素 $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$。其中，$k$ 和 $l$ 由表 7.4.3.1-1 给出，分别表示一个 SS/PBCH 块内的频率索引和时间索引。

**7.4.3.1.3 SS/PBCH 块内的 PBCH 和 DM-RS 映射（Mapping of PBCH and DM-RS within an SS/PBCH block）**

**原文**

The UE shall assume the sequence of complex-valued symbols $d_{\mathrm{PBCH}}(0),\ldots,d_{\mathrm{PBCH}}(M_{\mathrm{symb}}-1)$ constituting the physical broadcast channel to be scaled by a factor $\beta_{\mathrm{PBCH}}$ to conform to the PBCH power allocation specified in [5, TS 38.213] and mapped in sequence starting with $d_{\mathrm{PBCH}}(0)$ to resource elements $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$ which meet all the following criteria:

- they are not used for PBCH demodulation reference signals

**译文**

UE 应假定构成物理广播信道的复值符号序列 $d_{\mathrm{PBCH}}(0),\ldots,d_{\mathrm{PBCH}}(M_{\mathrm{symb}}-1)$ 乘以缩放因子 $\beta_{\mathrm{PBCH}}$，以满足 [5, TS 38.213] 规定的 PBCH 功率分配，并从 $d_{\mathrm{PBCH}}(0)$ 开始，依次映射到满足以下全部条件的资源元素 $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$：

- 这些资源元素未用于 PBCH 解调参考信号。

**原文**

The mapping to resource elements $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$ not reserved for PBCH DM-RS shall be in increasing order of first the index $k$ and then the index $l$, where $k$ and $l$ represent the frequency and time indices, respectively, within one SS/PBCH block and are given by Table 7.4.3.1-1.

**译文**

向未保留用于 PBCH DM-RS 的资源元素 $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$ 进行映射时，应按先索引 $k$ 递增、再索引 $l$ 递增的顺序进行。其中，$k$ 和 $l$ 分别表示一个 SS/PBCH 块内的频率索引和时间索引，由表 7.4.3.1-1 给出。

**原文**

The UE shall assume the sequence of complex-valued symbols $r(0),\ldots,r(143)$ constituting the demodulation reference signals for the SS/PBCH block to be scaled by a factor of $\beta_{\mathrm{PBCH}}^{\mathrm{DM-RS}}$ to conform to the PBCH power allocation specified in [5, TS 38.213] and to be mapped to resource elements $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$ in increasing order of first $k$ and then $l$ where $k$ and $l$ are given by Table 7.4.3.1-1 and represent the frequency and time indices, respectively, within one SS/PBCH block.

**译文**

UE 应假定构成 SS/PBCH 块解调参考信号的复值符号序列 $r(0),\ldots,r(143)$ 乘以缩放因子 $\beta_{\mathrm{PBCH}}^{\mathrm{DM-RS}}$，以满足 [5, TS 38.213] 规定的 PBCH 功率分配，并按先 $k$ 后 $l$ 递增的顺序映射到资源元素 $(k,l)_{p,\mu}$。其中，$k$ 和 $l$ 由表 7.4.3.1-1 给出，分别表示一个 SS/PBCH 块内的频率索引和时间索引。

##### 7.4.3.2 SS/PBCH 块的时域位置（Time location of an SS/PBCH block）

**原文**

The locations in the time domain where a UE shall monitor for a possible SS/PBCH block are described in clause 4.1 of [5, TS 38.213].

**译文**

UE 应监听可能存在的 SS/PBCH 块的时域位置，在 [5, TS 38.213] 第 4.1 节中描述。
